- **duel**: Use when testing through pairs of inverted development teams — a prover that asserts and builds, and a refuter that takes the exact opposite side and attacks it. Popper asymmetry: one refutation falls the claim, no amount of proof verifies it — a claim STANDS as not-yet-refuted, never as proven true.
